# Skills · N · part 12

901 entries, neqsim-stage-gate-project-timeline to nestjs-swagger-integration. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **neqsim-stage-gate-project-timeline**: Educational stage-gate project timelines for field-development screening: prospect maturation (exploration, appraisal, DG1-DG3, execution, first oil at a turnaround), host modification projects scheduled ASAP or just in time with cost in the phases that incur it, and host life-extension decision deadlines, combined into a portfolio plan, capex calendar and Gantt. USE WHEN: a tie-in, area-development or debottlenecking screening must respect that projects take time. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/field-development/neqsim-stage-gate-project-timeline) · ★5)
- **neqsim-standard-engineering-english**: Apply a concise controlled-language house style inspired by ASD-STE100 to final engineering documents and generated summaries. USE WHEN: the final reporting step produces procedures, equipment descriptions, technical requirements, warnings, cautions, checklists, method summaries, or other text that readers must interpret consistently, or when the user requests standard_engineering_english. Do not claim ASD-STE100 compliance. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/reporting/neqsim-standard-engineering-english) · ★5)
- **neqsim-standard-equipment-sizes**: Rounds calculated equipment sizes up to standard industry sizes - pipe NPS/DN, API 526 relief-valve orifices, ASME B16.5 flange classes, typical IEC motor ratings, exchanger tube lengths, vessel diameter steps - and defines the size-source precedence of project/company datasheet, vendor, then public standard. USE WHEN: a design or study produces a calculated diameter, area, power or rating that must be reported as a buildable standard size, or a report quotes an equipment size. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-standard-equipment-sizes) · ★171 · 2 copies)
- **neqsim-standard-requirement-extraction**: Extract and map engineering requirements from standards or approved standards excerpts into a structured format that can support NeqSim book chapters, engineering reports, and design checks. Use this for NORSOK, DNV, API, ISO, IEC, ASME, and similar standards when clause-level traceability is needed. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/neqsim-paperlab/skills/neqsim-standard-requirement-extraction) · ★171)
- **neqsim-standard-requirement-extraction**: Extract and map engineering requirements from standards or approved standards ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-standard-requirement-extraction) · ★171)
- **neqsim-standards-lookup**: Industry standards lookup and compliance tracking for NeqSim engineering tasks. USE WHEN: any engineering task requires standards compliance (API, ISO, NORSOK, DNV, ASME, EN, ASTM), ISO 18453 / GERG-water water dew point, risk assessment, or safety analysis. Provides equipment-to-standards mapping, calculation setup rules, database query patterns, results.json schema for standards_applied, and risk standards quick-reference. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-standards-lookup) · ★171)
- **neqsim-step-out-screening**: Educational subsea tie-back step-out and arrival-pressure screening with optional hydrate-margin input. USE WHEN: a task needs a public, screening-level check of whether a tie-back step-out distance and arrival pressure stay within simple guidelines before detailed flow-assurance and hydraulic design. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/subsea/neqsim-step-out-screening) · ★5)
- **neqsim-stid-retriever**: Retrieves engineering documents (compressor curves, drawings, line lists, P&IDs, datasheets, vendor docs, material certificates, fire/PFP documents, piping specs) from local folders, uploads or pluggable backends such as stidapi. USE WHEN: a task needs vendor performance data, mechanical drawings, line-list route hydraulics, water-hammer or trapped-liquid rupture evidence, or as-built documentation for process equipment. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-stid-retriever) · ★171)
- **neqsim-subsea-and-wells**: Subsea systems, DNV-RP-F109 on-bottom stability, DNV-RP-F105 free span, DNV-RP-F101 corroded pipe, well design, SURF cost and tieback analysis with NeqSim. USE WHEN: designing subsea fields, screening pipeline/cable/umbilical stability or metal loss, sizing flowlines and umbilicals, estimating well cost, casing design, comparing tiebacks, or configuring trees, manifolds, boosters and risers. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-subsea-and-wells) · ★171)
- **neqsim-subsea-equipment-placement**: Constraint-based siting of subsea equipment and routes (drill centres, templates, manifolds, PLETs, tie-ins): screens and ranks sites against clearances, existing lines and crossings, exclusion zones, seabed slope, fishing exposure and well reach, and checks the route. USE WHEN: a tie-back or SURF study must decide where equipment goes, or defend a placement against map data, standards and installation constraints. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-subsea-equipment-placement) · ★171 · 2 copies)
- **neqsim-subsea-layout-geometry**: Educational subsea field-layout geometry screening from supplied coordinates. USE WHEN: a task needs public, screening-level step-out distances, tie-back lengths, a node-to-node distance matrix, or a schematic plan-view illustration for wells, manifolds, templates, and a host from a subsea map before detailed routing and hydraulic design. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/subsea/neqsim-subsea-layout-geometry) · ★5)
- **neqsim-subsea-slot-tee-reuse-inventory**: Inventory of tie-in capacity in existing subsea infrastructure (free template/manifold slots, spare tees and hubs, J-tube/riser slots, spare or reusable pipelines, flowlines, gas-lift, injection and MEG lines, umbilical cores) with status, size, remaining life, source and verification level, and a reuse-versus-new-build ranking. USE WHEN: a tie-in needs to know which existing slots, tees or lines can be used or re-purposed, or which line could raise production. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/subsea/neqsim-subsea-slot-tee-reuse-inventory) · ★5)
- **neqsim-sulfur-recovery**: Guides NeqSim agents through sulfur-recovery models anchored on SulfurRecoveryUnit, SulfurRecoveryProcessBuilder, ClausReactionFurnace, ClausCatalyticConverter, and SulfurRecoveryPerformance. USE WHEN: building a Claus sulfur-recovery train, choosing straight-through, split-flow, or oxygen-enriched operation, adding tail-gas treatment and recycle, checking sulfur balance or recovery, tuning furnace/converter inputs, or estimating sulfur product and stack SO2. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-sulfur-recovery) · ★171)
- **neqsim-surf-cooldown-screening**: Educational SURF flowline/riser cooldown and no-touch-time screening placeholder with public assumptions. USE WHEN: a task needs a quick, public estimate of how long an insulated subsea flowline stays above its hydrate formation temperature after shutdown, and should be directed to validated NeqSim methods for real cooldown and hydrate calculations. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/flow-assurance/neqsim-surf-cooldown-screening) · ★5)
- **neqsim-surf-design-study**: End-to-end SURF (subsea umbilicals, risers, flowlines) design study: request plus structured inputs become a generated, solved NeqSim field network, concept comparison with feasibility before cost, checks with explicit maturity, drawings, MTO/cost and a validated report via `neqsim surf-study`. USE WHEN: a subsea tieback, template cluster, daisy chain, injection distribution or SURF design report is requested. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-surf-design-study) · ★171)
- **neqsim-surf-field-layout-design**: Design a screening SURF field layout and host position from open map, bathymetry and licence data: drill centres, trees, templates, manifolds, riser bases, routed and sized flowlines, umbilicals and risers, GeoJSON export and a reservoir-to-host cutaway. USE WHEN: a layout must be designed, not screened - template count, host location, loop/single/daisy-chain architecture, line sizes, umbilical lengths, or a georeferenced hand-off to flow assurance, cost or NeqSim network models. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/subsea/neqsim-surf-field-layout-design) · ★5)
- **neqsim-surf-model-generation**: Generates the executable NeqSim SURF model from a surf_study.v1 spec: FieldNetworkTopology over LoopedPipeNetwork, live WellFlow wells, FieldWellNetworkProcessUnit with a host separator, SubseaProductionSystem line designs, umbilical networks, ID mapping manifest and an editable Python builder. USE WHEN: a subsea network must be built from structured inputs, concepts compared, or a user-built model adopted without overwriting equipment. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-surf-model-generation) · ★171)
- **neqsim-surf-report-drawings**: SURF drawing set, datasheets, registers and 20-chapter report contract: 15 deterministic A3 sheets (field plan, network schematic, functional process sheets, route profiles, riser, template/PLEM, jumper, umbilical, thermal cross-sections, installation, results, comparison, quantities), canonical dataset tokens, fail-closed validation and visual inspection. USE WHEN: SURF drawings or the SURF design report are requested, reviewed or extended. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-surf-report-drawings) · ★171)
- **neqsim-task-grill**: USE WHEN: an engineering task is about to start and its basis is not settled - interview the user one question at a time, each with a recommended answer, on the decision or question, the quality bar, the available data and its sources, access to tools and data, constraints, open decisions and the weakest assumptions, then write a task brief, data requests and a run plan that names which NeqSim, community and enterprise agents produce which reports. Never invents data; unknown stays OPEN. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/reporting/neqsim-task-grill) · ★5)
- **neqsim-task-workflow**: The full NeqSim engineering task workflow that @solve-task orchestrates. USE WHEN: executing any Standard or Comprehensive task end to end - setup and scale classification, scope and research, deep analysis, notebooks with benchmark validation and uncertainty/risk, quality gates, report and work record, knowledge capture, task-type guidance, delegation, NeqSim Improvement Proposals and lessons learned. Load one phase section at a time. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-task-workflow) · ★171)
- **neqsim-technical-document-reading**: Extracts structured engineering data from documents (PDF, Word, Excel, CSV) and drawings/images (P&IDs, datasheets, arrangements, performance maps). USE WHEN: a user provides datasheets, technical requirements, design basis, well tests, inspection reports, standards, vendor drawings, compressor maps, phase envelopes, material certificates or rupture/water-hammer evidence and needs data for simulation. Covers classification, extraction patterns, view_image, units and quality scoring. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-technical-document-reading) · ★171)
- **neqsim-technology-assessment**: Educational technology assessment for new or step-out technology: decomposition, novelty categorisation (1-4), technology readiness level (TRL 0-7) roll-up, enabling vs enhancing, step-out, benefits and risks, standardisation potential and a preliminary qualification outline. USE WHEN: a project study must decide which technology elements need qualification before FMIRR, a qualification programme or a technology assessment report. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/field-development/neqsim-technology-assessment) · ★5)
- **neqsim-technology-assessment-report**: Builds a full technology assessment report (Markdown and Word) for any technology from technology_assessment.v1 and technology_fmirr.v1 results: qualification basis, decomposition, novelty and TRL, step-out, benefits and risks, FMIRR, qualification outline, conclusions, open points. USE WHEN: a project study needs a technology assessment report as a deliverable, or an assessment and FMIRR must be turned into a reviewable document. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/reporting/neqsim-technology-assessment-report) · ★5)
- **neqsim-technology-fmirr**: Educational failure-mode identification and risk ranking (FMIRR) register for any new technology: separate failed item, mode, mechanism and cause (ISO 14224 terms), 5x5 consequence-probability ranking, critical modes linked to qualification tests, re-rating history. USE WHEN: a technology assessment has identified novel elements and the project needs a ranked failure-mode register to define the qualification plan. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/safety/neqsim-technology-fmirr) · ★5)
- **neqsim-teg-dehydration-modeling**: Build a validated, closed-loop TEG (triethylene glycol) gas dehydration plant in NeqSim, including absorber, flash drum, regeneration column, stripper, lean-TEG recycle, and still-vent emission classification. USE WHEN: a task needs a runnable NeqSim TEG dehydration flowsheet (water dew point, lean-TEG purity, NMVOC/methane/benzene still-vent emissions) rather than a screening-level water-content estimate. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/process/neqsim-teg-dehydration-modeling) · ★5)
- **neqsim-thermodynamic-initialization**: Select and audit NeqSim thermodynamic initialization levels for correctness and performance. USE WHEN: implementing or reviewing flashes, phase stability, EOS root selection, process-equipment thermodynamics, property access, or performance changes involving init(...) or initProperties(). ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-thermodynamic-initialization) · ★171)
- **neqsim-thermoml-experimental-data**: Fetch published experimental thermophysical data (density, VLE, viscosity, solubility, heat capacity) from the NIST/TRC ThermoML Archive by DOI with no login, flatten it into state points with expanded uncertainties, and score a NeqSim model against it with AAD, bias and within-uncertainty statistics. USE WHEN: validating or tuning an EOS or kij against measured data, finding measurements for a binary or mixture, or backing a benchmark with citable experiments rather than another model. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pvt/neqsim-thermoml-experimental-data) · ★5)
- **neqsim-tie-in-pid-sketch**: Conceptual P&ID sketches (SVG) of both ends of a new tie-in line - source platform end with pump, metering, pig launcher, ESDV and riser; landing end with riser, ESDV, pig receiver, sampling, pressure control, spectacle blind and tie-in to an existing header - plus a completeness check. USE WHEN: a tie-in or export concept needs a drawing of possible connection points and the isolation, pigging and metering philosophy before a controlled P&ID is made. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/subsea/neqsim-tie-in-pid-sketch) · ★5)
- **neqsim-tie-in-pipeline-concept**: Screening of a new export or tie-in pipeline between two facilities: ranked landing points on neighbouring hosts, seabed profile from open EMODnet bathymetry, pressure drop, thermal decay, DNV-ST-F101 wall thickness and a recommended nominal size per rate case. USE WHEN: a task asks where an oil or gas line can be tied in, what size and wall thickness it needs, what inlet pressure a pump must deliver, or wants a pipe profile before a NeqSim pipeline study. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/subsea/neqsim-tie-in-pipeline-concept) · ★5)
- **neqsim-tight-reservoir-test-design**: Screening workflow for opening a tight (chalk, tight carbonate or sandstone) reservoir through an existing host: stimulated-horizontal-well rate and EUR with NeqSim PVT, Arps extrapolation bias, production-test duration and sampling, flowback chemistry and compatibility, value of information of the test and stage-gate criteria. USE WHEN: designing a production test, drainage strategy or stage-gate pipeline for a tight reservoir tied to an existing facility (chalk analogues). ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-tight-reservoir-test-design) · ★171)
- **neqsim-trapped-liquid-fire-rupture**: Fire rupture studies for blocked-in liquid-filled pipe segments and blowdown pipe heat-up / strain-rate time-to-rupture. USE WHEN: a task involves trapped or blocked-in liquid, thermal expansion rupture, fire exposure without relief, PFP demand, flange/pipe rupture screening, a supplied blowdown pressure profile, or a Word/HTML safety study from P&IDs, line lists, piping specs, material certificates and fire documents. Anchors on neqsim.process.safety.rupture. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-trapped-liquid-fire-rupture) · ★171)
- **neqsim-troubleshooting**: Troubleshoot oil and gas process operating problems and NeqSim simulation failures. Route measured plant disturbances to the root-cause agent. USE WHEN: a simulation fails to converge, produces unexpected results, throws exceptions, or gives zero/NaN values. Covers flash non-convergence, recycle divergence, equipment errors, phase identification issues, and numerical problems. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-troubleshooting) · ★171)
- **neqsim-two-phase-flow-regime-screening**: Educational two-phase flow-regime screening that classifies a horizontal gas-liquid flow pattern from superficial velocities using a simplified public Mandhane-style map and flags slug risk. USE WHEN: a task needs a public, screening-level flow-regime flag (stratified, slug, annular, bubble) and slug-risk triage to pair with slug-flow and flow-induced-vibration work. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/flow-assurance/neqsim-two-phase-flow-regime-screening) · ★5)
- **neqsim-umbilical-design**: Subsea umbilical sizing and drawings with the NeqSim umbilical physics: tube wall, cross-section and weights, armour from catenary tension, chemical and hydraulic line friction and response, power cable with charging current, design checks, figures and study hand-off. USE WHEN: a SURF or tie-back task needs an umbilical sized and drawn instead of a cost-library line item, or chemical injection, hydraulic response, power step-out or bend radius must be answered. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-umbilical-design) · ★171 · 2 copies)
- **neqsim-uncertainty-quantification**: Monte Carlo uncertainty, tornado and global sensitivity for NeqSim tasks: P10/P90 marginals, LHS/Halton sampling, a cached technical/economic split, ascending p10&lt;=p50&lt;=p90 percentiles, convergence check, optional SALib Sobol/Morris and chaospy surrogate, and the uncertainty block the report and CI gate consume. USE WHEN: reporting P10/P50/P90, a tornado or a probability of a negative outcome, wrapping an expensive simulation, ranking parameters, or auditing an uncertainty block. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/process/neqsim-uncertainty-quantification) · ★5)
- **neqsim-unisim-reader**: Reads Honeywell UniSim Design / Aspen HYSYS .usc files via COM and converts them to NeqSim ProcessSystem / ProcessModule models. USE WHEN: a user has UniSim/HYSYS files to recreate or compare in NeqSim. Covers COM navigation, column feed/product connectivity, component mapping, E300 fluid transfer, the operation-handler registry, topology and sub-flowsheets, batch regression against the sample library and result verification. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-unisim-reader) · ★171)
- **neqsim-utilities-design-report**: Develops a traceable process utilities design report for onshore plants and offshore facilities by extending process-study-report. USE WHEN: a utilities design report, utility balance, consumer register, utility flow diagrams, generation/distribution capacity assessment or utility-loss study is requested. Covers heating/steam, cooling/refrigeration, air/nitrogen, fuel gas, water, drains and safety/electrical interfaces; calculation methods remain in existing utility specialists. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-utilities-design-report) · ★171)
- **neqsim-utilities-specification**: USE WHEN: selecting and documenting process utility levels and quality requirements: steam, cooling/heating media, instrument air, fuel gas, nitrogen, water and refrigeration. Covers utility design bases, consumer-to-header allocation and project specification reviews. Use neqsim-utility-design for screening calculations and neqsim-utilities-design-report for the complete report workflow. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-utilities-specification) · ★171)
- **neqsim-utility-balance-screening**: Educational utility-balance screening for instrument air demand, fuel gas Wobbe index, and cooling water duty/flow with a simple capacity margin roll-up (NORSOK U-001 / ISA-7.0.01 style). USE WHEN: a task needs a public, screening-level estimate of instrument air demand, cooling water flow, fuel gas Wobbe index compliance, and utility capacity utilisation before detailed utility-system design. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/process/neqsim-utility-balance-screening) · ★5)
- **neqsim-utility-design**: Screening-level utility-system design with NeqSim and the MCP designUtilities tool - fired Boiler, Deaerator, RefrigerationCycle, NitrogenSystem, multi-pressure SteamNetwork and the UtilitySystemDesigner that harvests demands from a run flowsheet. USE WHEN: sizing a utility duty/flow/power, estimating utility CO2 and cost, building a steam header balance, or exposing utility sizing through MCP. Levels: neqsim-utilities-specification; duties: neqsim-heat-integration. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-utility-design) · ★171)
- **neqsim-vacuum-collapse-screening**: Educational vacuum-collapse (implosion) screening for a blocked-in vessel that cools down, with vacuum-depth and external-rating flags. USE WHEN: a task needs a public, screening-level check of whether a closed vessel cooldown or steam/vapour condensation can pull a vacuum below the external pressure rating, without proprietary vacuum-collapse design methods. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/safety/neqsim-vacuum-collapse-screening) · ★5)
- **neqsim-version-bump-decision**: USE WHEN: deciding whether a NeqSim project version request should be a patch or minor release. Classifies fixes, refactors, and additive features; checks Java serialization compatibility. Major-version decisions remain manual. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-version-bump-decision) · ★171)
- **neqsim-vfp-lift-curves**: Generate deliverable VFPPROD and VFPINJ lift curve tables (vertical lift performance, tubing performance, THP and BHP tables) for Eclipse and OPM Flow from calibrated well, flowline and topside models with NeqSim VfpTableBuilder, TubingVlpProvider, ForwardVlpProvider and EclipseVFPExporter. USE WHEN: a simulator needs lift curves, a VFP table is built from tubing, flowline or full-system models and validated by IPR x VFP, or an existing deck shows U-shaped BHP, extrapolation or a dry-gas jump. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-vfp-lift-curves) · ★171)
- **neqsim-water-dewpoint-dehydration-screening**: Educational gas water-content and dehydration screening using the public GPSA Bukacek saturated-water-content correlation. USE WHEN: a task needs a public, screening-level estimate of saturated water content in natural gas, a check against a sales-gas water spec, or a decision on whether a stated stream composition is already DEHYDRATED or merely cooled and knocked out (the saturation test), before detailed dehydration design or before asserting any hydrate/ice finding. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/process/neqsim-water-dewpoint-dehydration-screening) · ★5)
- **neqsim-water-hammer**: Water hammer and liquid hammer screening workflow for NeqSim. USE WHEN: evaluating fast valve closures, pump trips, check-valve slam, hydraulic surge, STID/P&ID route extraction, tagreader event windows, or MCP runWaterHammer studies. Covers WaterHammerPipe, WaterHammerStudy, STID route geometry, tagreader field-data overrides, pressure envelopes, Joukowsky checks, and screening limitations. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-water-hammer) · ★171)
- **neqsim-wax-calculations**: Use when: performing NeqSim wax calculations, wax appearance temperature (WAT), wax fraction versus temperature curves, wax precipitation, waxy-oil viscosity, wax model tuning, or wax inhibitor screening for flow assurance studies. Covers the verified Java and Python API setup for wax-forming oils with TBP and plus fractions. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/neqsim-wax-calculations) · ★171)
- **neqsim-wax-margin-check**: Educational wax operating-margin screening placeholder with public assumptions. USE WHEN: a task needs a quick check of whether an operating point keeps a safe temperature margin above a wax appearance temperature and should be directed to validated NeqSim methods for real calculations. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/flow-assurance/neqsim-wax-margin-check) · ★5)
- **neqsim-weather-data**: Live, forecast and historical weather, wind and sea-state data for installations from open, keyless APIs - Open-Meteo worldwide and MET Norway/Sodir for the Norwegian Continental Shelf. USE WHEN: a task needs current, forecast or historical temperature, wind, humidity, pressure or wave data for an installation - by name on the NCS or by coordinates anywhere - for gas-turbine derating, fire-water/flare wind screening, dispersion stability class, or subsea cooldown screening. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/environment/neqsim-weather-data) · ★5)
- **neqsim-well-design-study**: Screening well design study and well-plan document: pressure/fracture window, casing seats, kick tolerance, casing and tubing design (API TR 5C3) with load-basis comparison, torque and drag, ECD, offset calibration, time and cost P10/P50/P90, fail-closed validation, A3 well schematic, BOP and barrier sketches, Gantt, risk matrix and a professional Word report. USE WHEN: a concept or pre-FEED well design or well plan is needed, especially with offset wells. ([equinor/neqsim-community-skills](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim-community-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/subsurface/neqsim-well-design-study) · ★5)
- **neqsim-well-log-reading**: Read DLIS and LIS well logs with equinor/dlisio and LAS 1.2/2.0 with a built-in parser into one model: frames, curves, units, canonical mnemonics, nulls as NaN, depth in metres, curve inventory and SHA-256 provenance.
